It solves energy and mass balance equations to predict water temperature stratification.\n\n## Installation\nGLM is typically installed as a standalone executable:\n```bash\n# Check if GLM is installed\nwhich glm\n\n# Run GLM\nglm\n```\n\n## Configuration File (glm3.nml)\nGLM uses a Fortran namelist file (`.nml`) for configuration with the following key sections:\n\n### &glm_setup\nBasic simulation setup:\n- `sim_name`: Simulation identifier\n- `max_layers`: Maximum number of vertical layers\n- `min_layer_vol`: Minimum layer volume (m³)\n- `min_layer_thick`: Minimum layer thickness (m)\n- `max_layer_thick`: Maximum layer thickness (m)\n- `density_model`: Density calculation method (1 = UNESCO equation)\n\n### &time\nSimulation period:\n- `timefmt`: Time format (2 = 'YYYY-MM-DD hh:mm:ss')\n- `start`: Start date and time\n- `stop`: End date and time\n- `dt`: Time step in seconds (3600 = 1 hour)\n- `timezone`: Time zone offset from UTC\n\n### &morphometry\nLake physical characteristics:\n- `lake_name`: Lake identifier\n- `latitude`, `longitude`: Geographic coordinates\n- `bsn_len`, `bsn_wid`: Basin length and width (m)\n- `H`: Array of elevation values (m)\n- `A`: Array of corresponding surface areas (m²)\n\n### &meteorology\nWeather forcing data:\n- `meteo_fl`: Path to meteorological CSV file\n- `subdaily`: Use sub-daily data (true/false)\n- `wind_factor`, `sw_factor`, `lw_factor`: Scaling factors\n- `cd`, `ce`, `ch`: Drag coefficients\n\n### &mixing\nMixing parameterization:\n- `coef_mix_conv`: Convective mixing coefficient\n- `coef_wind_stir`: Wind stirring coefficient\n- `coef_mix_shear`: Shear mixing coefficient\n- `coef_mix_turb`: Turbulence coefficient\n- `coef_mix_KH`: Kelvin-Helmholtz mixing\n\n### &init_profiles\nInitial conditions:\n- `lake_depth`: Initial lake depth (m)\n- `num_depths`: Number of depth points\n- `the_depths`: Array of depth values (m)\n- `the_temps`: Array of initial temperatures (°C)\n\n### &inflow / &outflow\nStream inflows and outflows:\n- `num_inflows`: Number of inflow streams\n- `inflow_fl`: Paths to inflow CSV files\n- `inflow_vars`: Variables in inflow data (FLOW, TEMP, SALT)\n\n### &output\nOutput configuration:\n- `out_dir`: Output directory path\n- `out_fn`: Output file prefix\n- `nsave`: Save interval (time steps)\n\n## Running GLM\n```bash\n# Run from directory containing glm3.nml\nglm\n\n# Or specify config file\nglm --nml /path/to/glm3.nml\n```\n\n## Output Files\nGLM produces NetCDF format output:\n- `output.nc`: Main output file with temperature profiles\n- Variables typically include: temp, salt, density at different depths and times\n\n## Key Parameters for Calibration\nFor temperature simulation, the most sensitive parameters are:\n1. **Mixing coefficients**: `coef_mix_conv`, `coef_wind_stir`, `coef_mix_shear`\n2. **Light extinction**: `Kw` (light extinction coefficient)\n3. **Wind factor**: `wind_factor` (multiplier for wind speed)\n4. **Drag coefficients**: `cd`, `ce`, `ch`\n\n## Example Workflow\n1. Prepare meteorological forcing data (CSV format)\n2. Prepare inflow/outflow data (CSV format)\n3. Configure glm3.nml with appropriate parameters\n4. Run GLM simulation\n5. Extract and analyze output from NetCDF file\n6. Compare with observations and calibrate if needed\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":5,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1189,"cache_read_input_tokens":22817,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1189,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"01cbf94c-47a2-45fa-a73e-5dc9deb35b53","uuid":"fda67a13-3ed2-45fb-b356-99c5111e00c0"}

It's commonly used in climate, oceanography, and hydrological modeling.\n\n## Installation\n```bash\npip install netCDF4\n# Or\npip install xarray  # Higher-level interface\n```\n\n## Using netCDF4 Library\n\n### Reading NetCDF Files\n```python\nimport netCDF4 as nc\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Open NetCDF file\nds = nc.Dataset('output.nc', 'r')\n\n# List all variables\nprint(ds.variables.keys())\n\n# Get variable information\nprint(ds.variables['temp'])\n\n# Read variable data\ntemp = ds.variables['temp'][:]  # Returns numpy array\n\n# Get dimensions\ntime = ds.variables['time'][:]\ndepth = ds.variables['z'][:]\n\n# Access attributes\nunits = ds.variables['temp'].units\n\n# Close file\nds.close()\n```\n\n### Working with Time\n```python\n# NetCDF often stores time as \"days since YYYY-MM-DD\"\nimport netCDF4 as nc\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\nds = nc.Dataset('output.nc', 'r')\ntime_var = ds.variables['time']\ndates = nc.num2date(time_var[:], units=time_var.units)\n\n# Convert to datetime array\ntime_list = [datetime(d.year, d.month, d.day, d.hour, d.minute)\n             for d in dates]\n```\n\n### Extracting Data at Specific Depths\n```python\n# Temperature at specific depth over time\ntemp_at_5m = ds.variables['temp'][:, depth_index_5m]\n\n# Temperature profile at specific time\ntemp_profile = ds.variables['temp'][time_index, :]\n```\n\n## Using xarray (Recommended)\n\n### Reading with xarray\n```python\nimport xarray as xr\n\n# Open dataset\nds = xr.open_dataset('output.nc')\n\n# View structure\nprint(ds)\n\n# Access variables as DataArrays\ntemp = ds['temp']\n\n# Select data by value (not index)\ntemp_at_5m = ds['temp'].sel(z=5, method='nearest')\n\n# Select time range\ntemp_period = ds['temp'].sel(time=slice('2009-01-01', '2009-12-31'))\n\n# Convert to pandas DataFrame\ndf = temp.to_dataframe()\n\n# Close dataset\nds.close()\n```\n\n### Advantages of xarray\n- Label-based indexing (use actual values instead of indices)\n- Built-in time handling\n- Easy conversion to pandas\n- Lazy loading for large files\n- Intuitive syntax\n\n## Common Operations\n\n### Interpolation\n```python\nimport xarray as xr\n\nds = xr.open_dataset('output.nc')\n\n# Interpolate to specific depths\ntarget_depths = [0, 1, 2, 5, 10, 15, 20]\ntemp_interp = ds['temp'].interp(z=target_depths)\n```\n\n### Resampling Time\n```python\n# Daily average from hourly data\ntemp_daily = ds['temp'].resample(time='1D').mean()\n```\n\n### Computing Statistics\n```python\n# Mean over time\ntemp_mean = ds['temp'].mean(dim='time')\n\n# Standard deviation over depth\ntemp_std = ds['temp'].std(dim='z')\n```\n\n## Example: Extract Temperature at Observation Points\n```python\nimport xarray as xr\nimport pandas as pd\n\n# Load simulation output\nsim = row in obs.iterrows():\n    # Find nearest time and depth in simulation\n    sim_temp = sim['temp'].sel(\n        time=row['datetime'],\n        z=row['depth'],\n        method='nearest'\n    )\n This is essential for hydrological and environmental models.\n\n## Key Concepts\n\n### Objective Functions\nCommon metrics for comparing simulations to observations:\n\n1. **RMSE (Root Mean Square Error)**\n   - Measures average magnitude of error\n   - Units: same as measured variable\n   - Formula: `sqrt(mean((observed - simulated)²))`\n   - Lower is better; 0 = perfect fit\n\n2. **MAE (Mean Absolute Error)**\n   - Less sensitive to outliers than RMSE\n   - Formula: `mean(abs(observed - simulated))`\n\n3. **Nash-Sutcliffe Efficiency (NSE)**\n   - Ranges from -∞ to 1 (1 = perfect fit)\n   - Formula: `1 - sum((obs - sim)²) / sum((obs - mean(obs))²)`\n\n4. **R² (Coefficient of Determination)**\n   - Ranges from 0 to 1\n   - Measures proportion of variance explained\n\n## Calculating RMSE in Python\n\n```python\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\n\ndef calculate_rmse(observed, simulated):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate Root Mean Square Error\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    observed : array-like\n        Observed values\n    simulated : array-like\n        Simulated values\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    float : RMSE value\n    \"\"\"\n    # Remove NaN values\n    mask = ~(np.isnan(observed) | np.isnan(simulated))\n    obs_clean = np.array(observed)[mask]\n    sim_clean = np.array(simulated)[mask]\n\n    # Calculate RMSE\n    mse = np.mean((obs_clean - sim_clean) ** 2)\n    rmse = np.sqrt(mse)\n\n    return rmse\n\n# Example usage\nobs = [1.5, 2.3, 3.1, 4.2]\nsim = [1.6, 2.1, 3.3, 4.0]\nrmse = calculate_rmse(obs, sim)\nprint(f\"RMSE: {rmse:.2f}\")\n```\n\n## Optimization Methods\n\n### 1. Manual Calibration\nIteratively adjust parameters based on domain knowledge:\n```python\n# Example parameter ranges for lake models\nparam_ranges = {\n    'wind_factor': (0.5, 1.5),\n    'coef_mix_conv': (0.1, 0.5),\n    'coef_wind_stir': (0.1, 0.5),\n    'Kw': (0.1, 1.0)  # Light extinction\n}\n\n# Test different values\nbest_rmse = float('inf')\nbest_params = {}\n\nfor wind_factor in np.linspace(0.5, 1.5, 5):\n    # Update parameter in config\n    # Run model\n    # Calculate RMSE\n    if rmse < best_rmse:\n        best_rmse = rmse\n        best_params['wind_factor'] = wind_factor\n```\n\n### 2. Grid Search\nSystematic search over parameter space:\n```python\nimport itertools\n\ndef grid_search(param_grid, run_model_func, calc_error_func):\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform grid search over parameters\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    param_grid : dict\n        Dictionary of parameter names and value lists\n    run_model_func : callable\n        Function that runs model with given parameters\n    calc_error_func : callable\n        Function that calculates error metric\n    \"\"\"\n    best_score = float('inf')\n    best_params = None\n\n    # Generate all combinations\n    keys = param_grid.keys()\n    value_combinations = itertools.product(*param_grid.values())\n\n    for values in value_combinations:\n        params = dict(zip(keys, values))\n\n        # Run model with these parameters\n        simulation = run_model_func(params)\n\n        # Calculate error\n        error = calc_error_func(simulation)\n\n        if error < best_score:\n            best_score = error\n            best_params = params\n\n    return best_params, best_score\n```\n\n### 3. Scipy Optimization\nUsing scipy.optimize for more efficient calibration:\n```python\nfrom scipy.optimize import minimize, differential_evolution\n\ndef objective_function(params, *args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Objective function to minimize (e.g., RMSE)\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    params : array-like\n        Parameter values to test\n    args : tuple\n        Additional arguments (observations, model runner, etc.)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Extract parameters\n    wind_factor, coef_mix_conv, kw = params\n\n    # Run model with these parameters\n    simulation = run_model(wind_factor, coef_mix_conv, kw)\n\n    # Calculate RMSE\n    rmse = calculate_rmse(observations, simulation)\n\n    return rmse\n\n# Define bounds\nbounds = [\n    (0.5, 1.5),   # wind_factor\n    (0.1, 0.5),   # coef_mix_conv\n    (0.1, 1.0)    # Kw\n]\n\n# Initial guess\nx0 = [1.0, 0.2, 0.3]\n\n# Optimize using Nelder-Mead\nresult = minimize(\n    objective_function,\n    x0,\n    args=(observations,),\n    bounds=bounds,\n    method='Nelder-Mead'\n)\n\nprint(f\"Best parameters: {result.x}\")\nprint(f\"Best RMSE: {result.fun}\")\n\n# Alternative: Differential Evolution (global optimization)\nresult = differential_evolution(\n    objective_function,\n    bounds,\n    args=(observations,),\n    maxiter=100\n)\n```\n\n## Model Calibration Workflow\n\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\n\ndef calibrate_model(observations_file,\n                   param_ranges,\n                   run_model_func,\n                   target_rmse=2.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calibrate model to achieve target RMSE\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    observations_file : str\n        Path to observation data\n    param_ranges : dict\n        Parameter ranges to explore\n    run_model_func : callable\n        Function to run model\n    target_rmse : float\n        Target RMSE threshold\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    dict : Best parameters found\n    float : Achieved RMSE\n    \"\"\"\n    # Load observations\n    obs = pd.read_csv(observations_file)\n\n    # Initial parameters\n    current_params = {k: np.mean(v) for k, v in param_ranges.items()}\n\n    # Optimization loop\n    for iteration in range(100):\n        # Run model\n        sim_output = run_model_func(current_params)\n\n        # Calculate RMSE\n        rmse = calculate_rmse(obs['temp'], sim_output['temp'])\n\n        print(f\"Iteration {iteration}: RMSE = {rmse:.3f}\")\n\n        if rmse < target_rmse:\n            print(f\"Target RMSE achieved!\")\n            return current_params, rmse\n\n        # Update parameters (example: gradient-free method)\n        # ... optimization logic ...\n\n    return current_params, rmse\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Start with physical constraints**: Don't allow parameters outside physically reasonable ranges\n2. **Use domain knowledge**: Prioritize parameters known to be most sensitive\n3. **Multi-objective calibration**: Consider multiple metrics (RMSE, NSE, bias)\n4. **Validation**: Test calibrated parameters on independent data\n5. **Sensitivity analysis**: Understand which parameters matter most\n6. **Document**: Keep track of all parameter sets tested\n\n## Common Pitfalls\n- Over-fitting: Model fits calibration data but fails on validation data\n- Local minima: Optimization gets stuck at suboptimal parameter values\n- Equifinality: Multiple parameter sets give similar performance\n- Ignoring uncertainty: Not accounting for measurement errors in observations\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":5,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1189,"cache_read_input_tokens":22817,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1189,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"01cbf94c-47a2-45fa-a73e-5dc9deb35b53","uuid":"38093eac-a5c5-47f5-935d-d3ef3daff888"}

Data Loading and Exploration\n```python\n# Load observational data\nobs_df = pd.read_csv('observations.csv', parse_dates=['datetime'])\n\n# Inspect data\nprint(obs_df.info())\nprint(obs_df.describe())\nprint(f\"Date range: {obs_df['datetime'].min()} to {obs_df['datetime'].max()}\")\nprint(f\"Depths available: {sorted(obs_df['depth'].unique())}\")\n\n# Check for missing data\nmissing = obs_df.isnull().sum()\nprint(f\"Missing values:\\n{missing[missing > 0]}\")\n```\n\n### 2. Model Configuration\n```python\ndef update_nml_parameter(nml_file, section, param, value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Update a parameter in a Fortran namelist file\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    nml_file : str\n        Path to .nml file\n    section : str\n        Section name (e.g., 'mixing', 'meteorology')\n    param : str\n        Parameter name\n    value : float or str\n        New value\n    \"\"\"\n    with open(nml_file, 'r') as f:\n        lines = f.readlines()\n\n    in_section = False\n    updated_lines = []\n\n    for line in lines:\n        if f'&{section}' in line:\n            in_section = True\n        elif line.strip() == '/':\n            in_section = False\n        elif in_section and param in line and '=' in line:\n            # Update the parameter\n            indent = len(line) - len(line.lstrip())\n            line = ' ' * indent + f'{param} = {value}\\n'\n\n        updated_lines.append(line)\n\n    with open(nml_file, 'w') as f:\n        f.writelines(updated_lines)\n```\n\n### 3. Model Execution\n```python\nimport subprocess\nimport os\n\ndef run_model(config_file='glm3.nml', working_dir='.'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run a model executable\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    config_file : str\n        Configuration file name\n    working_dir : str\n        Directory to run model in\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    bool : True if successful\n    \"\"\"\n    original_dir = os.getcwd()\n\n    try:\n        os.chdir(working_dir)\n\n        # Run the model\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            ['glm'],\n            capture_output=True,\n            text=True,\n            timeout=300  # 5 minute timeout\n        )\n\n        if result.returncode != 0:\n            print(f\"Model run failed:\")\n            print(result.stderr)\n            return False\n\n        return True\n\n    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:\n        print(\"Model run timed out\")\n        return False\n\n    finally:\n        os.chdir(original_dir)\n```\n\n### 4. Output Processing\n```python\ndef extract_model_output(nc_file, obs_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Extract model output at observation points\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    nc_file : str\n        Path to NetCDF output file\n    obs_df : DataFrame\n        Observation data with 'datetime' and 'depth' columns\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    DataFrame : Observations with added 'simulated' column\n    \"\"\"\n    import xarray as xr\n    import pandas as pd\n\n    # Load model output\n    ds = xr.open_dataset(nc_file)\n\n    # Create copy of observations\n    result_df = obs_df.copy()\n\n    # Extract simulated values at each observation point\n    sim_values = []\n\n    for _, row in obs_df.iterrows():\n        try:\n            # Select nearest time and depth\n            sim_val = ds['temp'].sel(\n                time=pd.Timestamp(row['datetime']),\n                z=row['depth'],\n                method='nearest'\n            ).values\n\n            # Handle potential array/scalar issues\n            if isinstance(sim_val, np.ndarray):\n                sim_val = float(sim_val.item())\n            else:\n                sim_val = float(sim_val)\n\n            sim_values.append(sim_val)\n\n        except (KeyError, ValueError) as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Could not extract data for {row['datetime']} at {row['depth']}m\")\n            sim_values.append(np.nan)\n\n    result_df['simulated'] = sim_values\n    ds.close()\n\n    return result_df\n```\n\n### 5. Model Validation\n```python\ndef validate_model(obs_df, sim_col='simulated', obs_col='temp'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate validation metrics\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    obs_df : DataFrame\n        DataFrame with observed and simulated columns\n    sim_col : str\n        Name of simulation column\n    obs_col : str\n        Name of observation column\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    dict : Dictionary of metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    # Remove NaN values\n    valid_data = obs_df[[obs_col, sim_col]].dropna()\n\n    obs = valid_data[obs_col].values\n    sim = valid_data[sim_col].values\n\n    # Calculate metrics\n    rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((obs - sim) ** 2))\n    mae = np.mean(np.abs(obs - sim))\n    bias = np.mean(sim - obs)\n\n    # Nash-Sutcliffe Efficiency\n    nse = 1 - (np.sum((obs - sim) ** 2) /\n               np.sum((obs - np.mean(obs)) ** 2))\n\n    # R-squared\n    r_squared = np.corrcoef(obs, sim)[0, 1] ** 2\n\n    metrics = {\n        'RMSE': rmse,\n        'MAE': mae,\n        'Bias': bias,\n        'NSE': nse,\n        'R2': r_squared,\n        'N': len(valid_data)\n    }\n\n    return metrics\n```\n\n### 6. Visualization\n```python\ndef plot_comparison(obs_df, obs_col='temp', sim_col='simulated'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Create comparison plots\n    \"\"\"\n    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\n    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(12, 10))\n\n    # Time series plot\n    ax = axes[0, 0]\n    ax.plot(obs_df['datetime'], obs_df[obs_col], 'o',\n            label='Observed', alpha=0.5)\n    ax.plot(obs_df['datetime'], obs_df[sim_col], 'x',\n            label='Simulated', alpha=0.5)\n    ax.set_xlabel('Date')\n    ax.set_ylabel('Temperature (°C)')\n    ax.legend()\n    ax.set_title('Time Series')\n\n    # Scatter plot\n    ax = axes[0, 1]\n    ax.scatter(obs_df[obs_col], obs_df[sim_col], alpha=0.5)\n    lims = [min(obs_df[obs_col].min(), obs_df[sim_col].min()),\n            max(obs_df[obs_col].max(), obs_df[sim_col].max())]\n    ax.plot(lims, lims, 'r--', label='1:1 line')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Observed (°C)')\n    ax.set_ylabel('Simulated (°C)')\n    ax.legend()\n    ax.set_title('Scatter Plot')\n\n    # Residuals\n    ax = axes[1, 0]\n    residuals = obs_df[sim_col] - obs_df[obs_col]\n    ax.scatter(obs_df[obs_col], residuals, alpha=0.5)\n    ax.axhline(y=0, color='r', linestyle='--')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Observed (°C)')\n    ax.set_ylabel('Residual (°C)')\n    ax.set_title('Residual Plot')\n\n    # Depth profile\n    ax = axes[1, 1]\n    depth_stats = obs_df.groupby('depth').agg({\n        obs_col: 'mean',\n        sim_col: 'mean'\n    })\n    ax.plot(depth_stats[obs_col], depth_stats.index, 'o-', label='Observed')\n    ax.plot(depth_stats[sim_col], depth_stats.index, 'x-', label='Simulated')\n    ax.invert_yaxis()\n    ax.set_ylabel('Depth (m)')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Mean Temperature (°C)')\n    ax.legend()\n    ax.set_title('Mean Depth Profile')\n\n    plt.tight_layout()\n    plt.savefig('model_validation.png', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')\n    plt.close()\n```\n\n## Complete Example Workflow\n\n```python\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\nimport xarray as xr\nimport subprocess\nimport os\n\n# 1. Load observations\nobs = pd.read_csv('observations.csv', parse_dates=['datetime'])\nprint(f\"Loaded {len(obs)} observations\")\n\n# 2. RMSE = {metrics['RMSE']:.3f} < 2.0\")\nelse:\n    print(f\"\\nRMSE = {metrics['RMSE']:.3f} exceeds threshold of 2.0\")\n    print(\"Consider calibrating model parameters\")\n```\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":5,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1189,"cache_read_input_tokens":22817,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1189,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"01cbf94c-47a2-45fa-a73e-5dc9deb35b53","uuid":"c49c173e-c863-4bfd-88a1-b2f1ad0be911"}

This is essential for hydrological and environmental models.\n\n## Key Concepts\n\n### Objective Functions\nCommon metrics for comparing simulations to observations:\n\n1. **RMSE (Root Mean Square Error)**\n   - Measures average magnitude of error\n   - Units: same as measured variable\n   - Formula: `sqrt(mean((observed - simulated)²))`\n   - Lower is better; 0 = perfect fit\n\n2. **MAE (Mean Absolute Error)**\n   - Less sensitive to outliers than RMSE\n   - Formula: `mean(abs(observed - simulated))`\n\n3. **Nash-Sutcliffe Efficiency (NSE)**\n   - Ranges from -∞ to 1 (1 = perfect fit)\n   - Formula: `1 - sum((obs - sim)²) / sum((obs - mean(obs))²)`\n\n4. **R² (Coefficient of Determination)**\n   - Ranges from 0 to 1\n   - Measures proportion of variance explained\n\n## Calculating RMSE in Python\n\n```python\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\n\ndef calculate_rmse(observed, simulated):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate Root Mean Square Error\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    observed : array-like\n        Observed values\n    simulated : array-like\n        Simulated values\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    float : RMSE value\n    \"\"\"\n    # Remove NaN values\n    mask = ~(np.isnan(observed) | np.isnan(simulated))\n    obs_clean = np.array(observed)[mask]\n    sim_clean = np.array(simulated)[mask]\n\n    # Calculate RMSE\n    mse = np.mean((obs_clean - sim_clean) ** 2)\n    rmse = np.sqrt(mse)\n\n    return rmse\n\n# Example usage\nobs = [1.5, 2.3, 3.1, 4.2]\nsim = [1.6, 2.1, 3.3, 4.0]\nrmse = calculate_rmse(obs, sim)\nprint(f\"RMSE: {rmse:.2f}\")\n```\n\n## Optimization Methods\n\n### 1. Manual Calibration\nIteratively adjust parameters based on domain knowledge:\n```python\n# Example parameter ranges for lake models\nparam_ranges = {\n    'wind_factor': (0.5, 1.5),\n    'coef_mix_conv': (0.1, 0.5),\n    'coef_wind_stir': (0.1, 0.5),\n    'Kw': (0.1, 1.0)  # Light extinction\n}\n\n# Test different values\nbest_rmse = float('inf')\nbest_params = {}\n\nfor wind_factor in np.linspace(0.5, 1.5, 5):\n    # Update parameter in config\n    # Run model\n    # Calculate RMSE\n    if rmse < best_rmse:\n        best_rmse = rmse\n        best_params['wind_factor'] = wind_factor\n```\n\n### 2. Grid Search\nSystematic search over parameter space:\n```python\nimport itertools\n\ndef grid_search(param_grid, run_model_func, calc_error_func):\n    \"\"\"\n    Perform grid search over parameters\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    param_grid : dict\n        Dictionary of parameter names and value lists\n    run_model_func : callable\n        Function that runs model with given parameters\n    calc_error_func : callable\n        Function that calculates error metric\n    \"\"\"\n    best_score = float('inf')\n    best_params = None\n\n    # Generate all combinations\n    keys = param_grid.keys()\n    value_combinations = itertools.product(*param_grid.values())\n\n    for values in value_combinations:\n        params = dict(zip(keys, values))\n\n        # Run model with these parameters\n        simulation = run_model_func(params)\n\n        # Calculate error\n        error = calc_error_func(simulation)\n\n        if error < best_score:\n            best_score = error\n            best_params = params\n\n    return best_params, best_score\n```\n\n### 3. Scipy Optimization\nUsing scipy.optimize for more efficient calibration:\n```python\nfrom scipy.optimize import minimize, differential_evolution\n\ndef objective_function(params, *args):\n    \"\"\"\n    Objective function to minimize (e.g., RMSE)\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    params : array-like\n        Parameter values to test\n    args : tuple\n        Additional arguments (observations, model runner, etc.)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Extract parameters\n    wind_factor, coef_mix_conv, kw = params\n\n    # Run model with these parameters\n    simulation = run_model(wind_factor, coef_mix_conv, kw)\n\n    # Calculate RMSE\n    rmse = calculate_rmse(observations, simulation)\n\n    return rmse\n\n# Define bounds\nbounds = [\n    (0.5, 1.5),   # wind_factor\n    (0.1, 0.5),   # coef_mix_conv\n    (0.1, 1.0)    # Kw\n]\n\n# Initial guess\nx0 = [1.0, 0.2, 0.3]\n\n# Optimize using Nelder-Mead\nresult = minimize(\n    objective_function,\n    x0,\n    args=(observations,),\n    bounds=bounds,\n    method='Nelder-Mead'\n)\n\nprint(f\"Best parameters: {result.x}\")\nprint(f\"Best RMSE: {result.fun}\")\n\n# Alternative: Differential Evolution (global optimization)\nresult = differential_evolution(\n    objective_function,\n    bounds,\n    args=(observations,),\n    maxiter=100\n)\n```\n\n## Model Calibration Workflow\n\n```python\nimport pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\n\ndef calibrate_model(observations_file,\n                   param_ranges,\n                   run_model_func,\n                   target_rmse=2.0):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calibrate model to achieve target RMSE\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    observations_file : str\n        Path to observation data\n    param_ranges : dict\n        Parameter ranges to explore\n    run_model_func : callable\n        Function to run model\n    target_rmse : float\n        Target RMSE threshold\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    dict : Best parameters found\n    float : Achieved RMSE\n    \"\"\"\n    # Load observations\n    obs = pd.read_csv(observations_file)\n\n    # Initial parameters\n    current_params = {k: np.mean(v) for k, v in param_ranges.items()}\n\n    # Optimization loop\n    for iteration in range(100):\n        # Run model\n        sim_output = run_model_func(current_params)\n\n        # Calculate RMSE\n        rmse = calculate_rmse(obs['temp'], sim_output['temp'])\n\n        print(f\"Iteration {iteration}: RMSE = {rmse:.3f}\")\n\n        if rmse < target_rmse:\n            print(f\"Target RMSE achieved!\")\n            return current_params, rmse\n\n        # Update parameters (example: gradient-free method)\n        # ... optimization logic ...\n\n    return current_params, rmse\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Start with physical constraints**: Don't allow parameters outside physically reasonable ranges\n2. **Use domain knowledge**: Prioritize parameters known to be most sensitive\n3. **Multi-objective calibration**: Consider multiple metrics (RMSE, NSE, bias)\n4. **Validation**: Test calibrated parameters on independent data\n5. **Sensitivity analysis**: Understand which parameters matter most\n6. **Document**: Keep track of all parameter sets tested\n\n## Common Pitfalls\n- Over-fitting: Model fits calibration data but fails on validation data\n- Local minima: Optimization gets stuck at suboptimal parameter values\n- Equifinality: Multiple parameter sets give similar performance\n- Ignoring uncertainty: Not accounting for measurement errors in observations\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Grouping and Aggregation\n\n### GroupBy Operations\n```python\n# Group by depth and calculate mean temperature\ndepth_means = df.groupby('depth')['temp'].mean()\n\n# Multiple aggregations\nstats = df.groupby('depth').agg({\n    'temp': ['mean', 'std', 'min', 'max'],\n    'OXY_oxy': 'mean'\n})\n\n# Group by multiple columns\nmonthly_depth = df.groupby([df.index.month, 'depth'])['temp'].mean()\n```\n\n## Merging and Joining\n\n### Combining DataFrames\n```python\n# Merge on common column\nmerged = pd.merge(obs_df, sim_df, on=['datetime', 'depth'])\n\n# Merge with different column names\nmerged = pd.merge(obs_df, sim_df,\n                 left_on='time', right_on='datetime')\n\n# Outer join (keep all rows)\nmerged = pd.merge(obs_df, sim_df,\n                 on='datetime', how='outer')\n\n# Concatenate vertically\ncombined = pd.concat([df1, df2], ignore_index=True)\n\n# Concatenate horizontally\nside_by_side = pd.concat([df1, df2], axis=1)\n```\n\n## Data Cleaning\n\n### Handling Missing Values\n```python\n# Drop rows with any missing values\ndf_clean = df.dropna()\n\n# Drop rows where specific column is missing\ndf_clean = df.dropna(subset=['temp'])\n\n# Fill missing values\ndf['temp'].fillna(method='ffill')  # Forward fill\ndf['temp'].fillna(0)  # Fill with constant\ndf['temp'].fillna(df['temp'].mean())  # Fill with mean\n\n# Interpolate\ndf['temp'] = df['temp'].interpolate(method='linear')\n```\n\n### Removing Duplicates\n```python\n# Remove duplicate rows\ndf_unique = df.drop_duplicates()\n\n# Remove duplicates based on specific columns\ndf_unique = df.drop_duplicates(subset=['datetime', 'depth'])\n\n# Keep last occurrence instead of first\ndf_unique = df.drop_duplicates(keep='last')\n```\n\n## Applying Functions\n\n### Apply and Map\n```python\n# Apply function to column\ndf['temp_f'] = df['temp'].apply(lambda x: x * 9/5 + 32)\n\n# Apply function to entire row\ndef classify_temp(row):\n    if row['temp'] < 5:\n        return 'cold'\n    elif row['temp'] < 15:\n        return 'moderate'\n    else:\n        return 'warm'\n\ndf['temp_class'] = df.apply(classify_temp, axis=1)\n\n# Vectorized operations (faster)\ndf['temp_f'] = df['temp'] * 9/5 + 32\n```\n\n## Pivot Tables\n\n### Reshaping Data\n```python\n# Create pivot table\npivot = df.pivot_table(\n    values='temp',\n    index='datetime',\n    columns='depth',\n    aggfunc='mean'\n)\n\n# Pivot without aggregation (requires unique index/column pairs)\npivot = df.pivot(\n    index='datetime',\n    columns='depth',\n    values='temp'\n)\n\n# Melt (unpivot) - long to wide format\nmelted = pivot.reset_index().melt(\n    id_vars=['datetime'],\n    var_name='depth',\n    value_name='temp'\n)\n```\n\n## Export Data\n\n### Writing to Files\n```python\n# Write to CSV\ndf.to_csv('output.csv', index=False)\n\n# Write with specific formatting\ndf.to_csv('output.csv',\n          index=False,\n          float_format='%.2f',\n          date_format='%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')\n\n# Write Excel\ndf.to_excel('output.xlsx', sheet_name='Data', index=False)\n```\n\n## Example: Processing Lake Temperature Data\n\n```python\nimport pandas Data Loading and Exploration\n```python\n# Load observational data\nobs_df = pd.read_csv('observations.csv', parse_dates=['datetime'])\n\n# Inspect data\nprint(obs_df.info())\nprint(obs_df.describe())\nprint(f\"Date range: {obs_df['datetime'].min()} to {obs_df['datetime'].max()}\")\nprint(f\"Depths available: {sorted(obs_df['depth'].unique())}\")\n\n# Check for missing data\nmissing = obs_df.isnull().sum()\nprint(f\"Missing values:\\n{missing[missing > 0]}\")\n```\n\n### 2. Model Configuration\n```python\ndef update_nml_parameter(nml_file, section, param, value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Update a parameter in a Fortran namelist file\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    nml_file : str\n        Path to .nml file\n    section : str\n        Section name (e.g., 'mixing', 'meteorology')\n    param : str\n        Parameter name\n    value : float or str\n        New value\n    \"\"\"\n    with open(nml_file, 'r') as f:\n        lines = f.readlines()\n\n    in_section = False\n    updated_lines = []\n\n    for line in lines:\n        if f'&{section}' in line:\n            in_section = True\n        elif line.strip() == '/':\n            in_section = False\n        elif in_section and param in line and '=' in line:\n            # Update the parameter\n            indent = len(line) - len(line.lstrip())\n            line = ' ' * indent + f'{param} = {value}\\n'\n\n        updated_lines.append(line)\n\n    with open(nml_file, 'w') as f:\n        f.writelines(updated_lines)\n```\n\n### 3. Model Execution\n```python\nimport subprocess\nimport os\n\ndef run_model(config_file='glm3.nml', working_dir='.'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Run a model executable\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    config_file : str\n        Configuration file name\n    working_dir : str\n        Directory to run model in\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    bool : True if successful\n    \"\"\"\n    original_dir = os.getcwd()\n\n    try:\n        os.chdir(working_dir)\n\n        # Run the model\n        result = subprocess.run(\n            ['glm'],\n            capture_output=True,\n            text=True,\n            timeout=300  # 5 minute timeout\n        )\n\n        if result.returncode != 0:\n            print(f\"Model run failed:\")\n            print(result.stderr)\n            return False\n\n        return True\n\n    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:\n        print(\"Model run timed out\")\n        return False\n\n    finally:\n        os.chdir(original_dir)\n```\n\n### 4. Output Processing\n```python\ndef extract_model_output(nc_file, obs_df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Extract model output at observation points\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    nc_file : str\n        Path to NetCDF output file\n    obs_df : DataFrame\n        Observation data with 'datetime' and 'depth' columns\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    DataFrame : Observations with added 'simulated' column\n    \"\"\"\n    import xarray as xr\n    import pandas as pd\n\n    # Load model output\n    ds = xr.open_dataset(nc_file)\n\n    # Create copy of observations\n    result_df = obs_df.copy()\n\n    # Extract simulated values at each observation point\n    sim_values = []\n\n    for _, row in obs_df.iterrows():\n        try:\n            # Select nearest time and depth\n            sim_val = ds['temp'].sel(\n                time=pd.Timestamp(row['datetime']),\n                z=row['depth'],\n                method='nearest'\n            ).values\n\n            # Handle potential array/scalar issues\n            if isinstance(sim_val, np.ndarray):\n                sim_val = float(sim_val.item())\n            else:\n                sim_val = float(sim_val)\n\n            sim_values.append(sim_val)\n\n        except (KeyError, ValueError) as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Could not extract data for {row['datetime']} at {row['depth']}m\")\n            sim_values.append(np.nan)\n\n    result_df['simulated'] = sim_values\n    ds.close()\n\n    return result_df\n```\n\n### 5. Model Validation\n```python\ndef validate_model(obs_df, sim_col='simulated', obs_col='temp'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate validation metrics\n\n    Parameters:\n    -----------\n    obs_df : DataFrame\n        DataFrame with observed and simulated columns\n    sim_col : str\n        Name of simulation column\n    obs_col : str\n        Name of observation column\n\n    Returns:\n    --------\n    dict : Dictionary of metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    # Remove NaN values\n    valid_data = obs_df[[obs_col, sim_col]].dropna()\n\n    obs = valid_data[obs_col].values\n    sim = valid_data[sim_col].values\n\n    # Calculate metrics\n    rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((obs - sim) ** 2))\n    mae = np.mean(np.abs(obs - sim))\n    bias = np.mean(sim - obs)\n\n    # Nash-Sutcliffe Efficiency\n    nse = 1 - (np.sum((obs - sim) ** 2) /\n               np.sum((obs - np.mean(obs)) ** 2))\n\n    # R-squared\n    r_squared = np.corrcoef(obs, sim)[0, 1] ** 2\n\n    metrics = {\n        'RMSE': rmse,\n        'MAE': mae,\n        'Bias': bias,\n        'NSE': nse,\n        'R2': r_squared,\n        'N': len(valid_data)\n    }\n\n    return metrics\n```\n\n### 6. Visualization\n```python\ndef plot_comparison(obs_df, obs_col='temp', sim_col='simulated'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Create comparison plots\n    \"\"\"\n    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\n    fig, axes = plt.subplots(2, 2, figsize=(12, 10))\n\n    # Time series plot\n    ax = axes[0, 0]\n    ax.plot(obs_df['datetime'], obs_df[obs_col], 'o',\n            label='Observed', alpha=0.5)\n    ax.plot(obs_df['datetime'], obs_df[sim_col], 'x',\n            label='Simulated', alpha=0.5)\n    ax.set_xlabel('Date')\n    ax.set_ylabel('Temperature (°C)')\n    ax.legend()\n    ax.set_title('Time Series')\n\n    # Scatter plot\n    ax = axes[0, 1]\n    ax.scatter(obs_df[obs_col], obs_df[sim_col], alpha=0.5)\n    lims = [min(obs_df[obs_col].min(), obs_df[sim_col].min()),\n            max(obs_df[obs_col].max(), obs_df[sim_col].max())]\n    ax.plot(lims, lims, 'r--', label='1:1 line')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Observed (°C)')\n    ax.set_ylabel('Simulated (°C)')\n    ax.legend()\n    ax.set_title('Scatter Plot')\n\n    # Residuals\n    ax = axes[1, 0]\n    residuals = obs_df[sim_col] - obs_df[obs_col]\n    ax.scatter(obs_df[obs_col], residuals, alpha=0.5)\n    ax.axhline(y=0, color='r', linestyle='--')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Observed (°C)')\n    ax.set_ylabel('Residual (°C)')\n    ax.set_title('Residual Plot')\n\n    # Depth profile\n    ax = axes[1, 1]\n    depth_stats = obs_df.groupby('depth').agg({\n        obs_col: 'mean',\n        sim_col: 'mean'\n    })\n    ax.plot(depth_stats[obs_col], depth_stats.index, 'o-', label='Observed')\n    ax.plot(depth_stats[sim_col], depth_stats.index, 'x-', label='Simulated')\n    ax.invert_yaxis()\n    ax.set_ylabel('Depth (m)')\n    ax.set_xlabel('Mean Temperature (°C)')\n    ax.legend()\n    ax.set_title('Mean Depth Profile')\n\n    plt.tight_layout()\n    plt.savefig('model_validation.png', dpi=300, bbox_inches='tight')\n    plt.close()\n```\n\n## Complete Example Workflow\n\n```python\nimport numpy as np\nimport pandas as pd\nimport xarray as xr\nimport subprocess\nimport os\n\n# 1. Remove NaN values\n    mask = ~(np.isnan(observed) | np.isnan(simulated))\n    obs_clean = np.array(observed)[mask]\n    sim_clean = np.array(simulated)[mask]\n\n    if len(obs_clean) == 0:\n        return np.nan\n\n    # Calculate metrics\n    rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((obs - sim) ** 2))\n    mae = np.mean(np.abs(obs - sim))\n    bias = np.mean(sim - obs)\n\n    # Nash-Sutcliffe Efficiency\n    nse = 1 - (np.sum((obs - sim) ** 2) / np.sum((obs - np.mean(obs)) ** 2))\n\n    # R-squared\n    r_squared = np.corrcoef(obs, sim)[0, 1] ** 2\n\n    metrics = {\n        'RMSE': rmse,\n        'MAE': mae,\n        'Bias': bias,\n        'NSE': nse,\n        'R2': r_squared,\n        'N': len(valid_data)\n    }\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef update_nml_parameter(nml_file, section, param, value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Update a parameter in a Fortran namelist file\n\n    Parameters:\n        nml_file: Path to .nml file\n        section: Section name (e.g., 'mixing')\n        param: Parameter name\n        value: New value\n    \"\"\"\n    with open(nml_file, 'r') as f:\n        lines = f.readlines()\n\n    in_section = False\n    updated_lines = []\n    param_found = False\n\n    for line in lines:\n        if f'&{section}' in line:\n            in_section = True\n        elif line.strip() == '/':\n            in_section = False\n        elif in_section and param in line.split('=')[0]:\n            # Update the parameter\n            indent = len(line) - len(line.lstrip())\n            line = ' ' * indent + f'{param} = {value}\\n'\n            param_found = True\n\n        updated_lines.append(line)\n\n    if param_found:\n        with open(nml_file, 'w') as f:\n            f.writelines(updated_lines)\n        print(f\"Updated {section}/{param} = {value}\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"Warning: Parameter {param} not found in section {section}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"GLM Simulation for Lake Mendota\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # Load model output using netCDF4\n    ds = nc.Dataset(nc_file, 'r')\n\n    print(f\"NetCDF dimensions: {list(ds.dimensions.keys())}\")\n    print(f\"NetCDF variables: {list(ds.variables.keys())}\")\n\n    # Get time data\n    time_var = ds.variables['time']\n    time_units = time_var.units\n    time_values = time_var[:]\n\n    # Convert to datetime\n    sim_times = nc.num2date(time_values, units=time_units)\n    sim_times = [pd.Timestamp(t) for t in sim_times]\n\n    print(f\"Time range: {min(sim_times)} to {max(sim_times)}\")\n    print(f\"Number of time steps: {len(sim_times)}\")\n\n    # Get depth and temperature data\n    z_values = ds.variables['z'][:]  # Shape: (time, z, lat, lon)\n    temp_values = ds.variables['temp'][:]  # Shape: (time, z, lat, lon)\n    ns_values = ds.variables['NS'][:]  # Number of active layers at each time\n\n    print(f\"Temperature array shape: {temp_values.shape}\")\n    print(f\"Z array shape: {z_values.shape}\")\n\n    # Create copy of observations\n    result_df = obs_df.copy()\n    sim_values = []\n\n    print(f\"Extracting simulated values for {len(obs_df)} observations...\")\n\n    for idx, row in obs_df.iterrows():\n        try:\n            obs_time = pd.Timestamp(row['datetime'])\n            obs_depth = row['depth']\n\n            # Find nearest time index\n            time_diffs = [abs((st - obs_time).total_seconds()) for st in sim_times]\n            time_idx = np.argmin(time_diffs)\n\n            # Get depths at this time step\n            n_layers = int(ns_values[time_idx, 0, 0])\n            depths_at_time = z_values[time_idx, :n_layers, 0, 0]\n            temps_at_time = temp_values[time_idx, :n_layers, 0, 0]\n\n            # Find nearest depth\n            depth_diffs = np.abs(depths_at_time - obs_depth)\n            depth_idx = np.argmin(depth_diffs)\n\n            sim_temp = float(temps_at_time[depth_idx])\n            sim_values.append(sim_temp)\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Could not extract data for {row['datetime']} at {row['depth']}m: {e}\")\n            sim_values.append(np.nan)\n\n    result_df['simulated'] = sim_values\n    ds.close()\n\n    # Load model output\n    ds = xr.open_dataset(nc_file)\n\n    print(f\"NetCDF variables: {list(ds.variables.keys())}\")\n    print(f\"Time range: {ds.time.min().values} to {ds.time.max().values}\")\n\n    # Determine depth variable name (might be 'z' or 'NS')\n    if 'z' in ds.variables:\n        depth_var = 'z'\n    elif 'NS' in ds.variables:\n        depth_var = 'NS'\n    else:\n        print(f\"Available variables: {list(ds.variables.keys())}\")\n        raise ValueError(\"Could not find depth variable in NetCDF file\")\n\n    print(f\"Using depth variable: {depth_var}\")\n    print(f\"Depth range: {ds[depth_var].min().values} to {ds[depth_var].max().values}\")\n\n    # Create copy of observations\n    result_df = obs_df.copy()\n    sim_values = []\n\n    print(f\"Extracting simulated values for {len(obs_df)} observations...\")\n\n    for idx, row in obs_df.iterrows():\n        try:\n            # Convert datetime to pandas Timestamp\n            obs_time = pd.Timestamp(row['datetime'])\n            obs_depth = row['depth']\n\n            # Select nearest time and depth\n            sim_val = ds['temp'].sel(\n                time=obs_time,\n                **{depth_var: obs_depth},\n                method='nearest'\n            ).values\n\n            # Convert to float\n            if isinstance(sim_val, np.ndarray):\n                sim_val = float(sim_val.item())\n            else:\n                sim_val = float(sim_val)\n\n            sim_values.append(sim_val)\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Could not extract data for {row['datetime']} at {row['depth']}m: {e}\")\n            sim_values.append(np.nan)\n\n    result_df['simulated'] = sim_values\n    ds.close()\n\n    # Remove NaN values\n    valid_count = result_df['simulated'].notna().sum()\n    print(f\"Successfully extracted {valid_count}/{len(obs_df)} simulated values\")\n\n    return result_df\n\n\ndef calculate_rmse(observed, simulated):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate Root Mean Square Error\n\n    Parameters:\n        observed: Array of observed values\n        simulated: Array of simulated values\n\n    Returns:\n        float: RMSE value\n    \"\"\"\n    # Remove NaN values\n    mask = ~(np.isnan(observed) | np.isnan(simulated))\n    obs_clean = np.array(observed)[mask]\n    sim_clean = np.array(simulated)[mask]\n\n    if len(obs_clean) == 0:\n        return np.nan\n\n    # Calculate RMSE\n    mse = np.mean((obs_clean - sim_clean) ** 2)\n    rmse = np.sqrt(mse)\n\n    return rmse\n\n\ndef calculate_metrics(obs_df, obs_col='temp', sim_col='simulated'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate comprehensive validation metrics\n\n    Returns:\n        dict: Dictionary of metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    # Remove NaN values\n    valid_data = obs_df[[obs_col, sim_col]].dropna()\n\n    if len(valid_data) == 0:\n        print(\"No valid data for metric calculation\")\n        return None\n\n    obs = valid_data[obs_col].values\n    sim = valid_data[sim_col].values\n\n    # Calculate metrics\n    rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((obs - sim) ** 2))\n    mae = np.mean(np.abs(obs - sim))\n    bias = np.mean(sim - obs)\n\n    # Nash-Sutcliffe Efficiency\n    nse = 1 - (np.sum((obs - sim) ** 2) / np.sum((obs - np.mean(obs)) ** 2))\n\n    # R-squared\n    r_squared = np.corrcoef(obs, sim)[0, 1] ** 2\n\n    metrics = {\n        'RMSE': rmse,\n        'MAE': mae,\n        'Bias': bias,\n        'NSE': nse,\n        'R2': r_squared,\n        'N': len(valid_data)\n    }\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef update_nml_parameter(nml_file, section, param, value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Update a parameter in a Fortran namelist file\n\n    Parameters:\n        nml_file: Path to .nml file\n        section: Section name (e.g., 'mixing')\n        param: Parameter name\n        value: New value\n    \"\"\"\n    with open(nml_file, 'r') as f:\n        lines = f.readlines()\n\n    in_section = False\n    updated_lines = []\n    param_found = False\n\n    for line in lines:\n        if f'&{section}' in line:\n            in_section = True\n        elif line.strip() == '/':\n            in_section = False\n        elif in_section and param in line.split('=')[0]:\n            # Update the parameter\n            indent = len(line) - len(line.lstrip())\n            line = ' ' * indent + f'{param} = {value}\\n'\n            param_found = True\n\n        updated_lines.append(line)\n\n    if param_found:\n        with open(nml_file, 'w') as f:\n            f.writelines(updated_lines)\n        print(f\"Updated {section}/{param} = {value}\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"Warning: Parameter {param} not found in section {section}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"GLM Simulation for Lake Mendota\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # Check if output file exists\n    if not os.path.exists(output_nc):\n        print(f\"ERROR: Output file not found at {output_nc}\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    print(f\"Output file created: {output_nc}\")\n\n    # Extract simulated values at observation points\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\n    results_df = extract_simulation_at_observations(output_nc, obs_df)\n\n    # Calculate validation metrics\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"VALIDATION METRICS\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    metrics = calculate_metrics(results_df)\n\n    if metrics is None:\n        print(\"ERROR: Could not calculate metrics\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    print(f\"Number of valid comparisons: {metrics['N']}\")\n    print(f\"RMSE:  {metrics['RMSE']:.3f} °C\")\n    print(f\"MAE:   {metrics['MAE']:.3f} °C\")\n    print(f\"Bias:  {metrics['Bias']:.3f} °C\")\n    print(f\"NSE:   {metrics['NSE']:.3f}\")\n    print(f\"R²:    {metrics['R2']:.3f}\")\n\n    # Get time data\n    time_var = ds.variables['time']\n    time_units = time_var.units\n    time_values = time_var[:]\n\n    # Convert to datetime\n    sim_times = nc.num2date(time_values, units=time_units)\n    sim_times = [pd.Timestamp(t) for t in sim_times]\n\n    print(f\"Time range: {min(sim_times)} to {max(sim_times)}\")\n    print(f\"Number of time steps: {len(sim_times)}\")\n\n    # Get depth and temperature data\n    z_values = ds.variables['z'][:]  # Shape: (time, z, lat, lon)\n    temp_values = ds.variables['temp'][:]  # Shape: (time, z, lat, lon)\n    ns_values = ds.variables['NS'][:]  # Number of active layers at each time\n\n    print(f\"Temperature array shape: {temp_values.shape}\")\n    print(f\"Z array shape: {z_values.shape}\")\n\n    # Create copy of observations\n    result_df = obs_df.copy()\n    sim_values = []\n\n    print(f\"Extracting simulated values for {len(obs_df)} observations...\")\n\n    for idx, row in obs_df.iterrows():\n        try:\n            obs_time = pd.Timestamp(row['datetime'])\n            obs_depth = row['depth']\n\n            # Find nearest time index\n            time_diffs = [abs((st - obs_time).total_seconds()) for st in sim_times]\n            time_idx = np.argmin(time_diffs)\n\n            # Get depths at this time step\n            n_layers = int(ns_values[time_idx, 0, 0])\n            depths_at_time = z_values[time_idx, :n_layers, 0, 0]\n            temps_at_time = temp_values[time_idx, :n_layers, 0, 0]\n\n            # Find nearest depth\n            depth_diffs = np.abs(depths_at_time - obs_depth)\n            depth_idx = np.argmin(depth_diffs)\n\n            sim_temp = float(temps_at_time[depth_idx])\n            sim_values.append(sim_temp)\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"Warning: Could not extract data for {row['datetime']} at {row['depth']}m: {e}\")\n            sim_values.append(np.nan)\n\n    result_df['simulated'] = sim_values\n    ds.close()\n\n    # Remove NaN values\n    valid_count = result_df['simulated'].notna().sum()\n    print(f\"Successfully extracted {valid_count}/{len(obs_df)} simulated values\")\n\n    return result_df\n\n\ndef calculate_rmse(observed, simulated):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate Root Mean Square Error\n\n    Parameters:\n        observed: Array of observed values\n        simulated: Array of simulated values\n\n    Returns:\n        float: RMSE value\n    \"\"\"\n    # Remove NaN values\n    mask = ~(np.isnan(observed) | np.isnan(simulated))\n    obs_clean = np.array(observed)[mask]\n    sim_clean = np.array(simulated)[mask]\n\n    if len(obs_clean) == 0:\n        return np.nan\n\n    # Calculate RMSE\n    mse = np.mean((obs_clean - sim_clean) ** 2)\n    rmse = np.sqrt(mse)\n\n    return rmse\n\n\ndef calculate_metrics(obs_df, obs_col='temp', sim_col='simulated'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate comprehensive validation metrics\n\n    Returns:\n        dict: Dictionary of metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    # Remove NaN values\n    valid_data = obs_df[[obs_col, sim_col]].dropna()\n\n    if len(valid_data) == 0:\n        print(\"No valid data for metric calculation\")\n        return None\n\n    obs = valid_data[obs_col].values\n    sim = valid_data[sim_col].values\n\n    # Calculate metrics\n    rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((obs - sim) ** 2))\n    mae = np.mean(np.abs(obs - sim))\n    bias = np.mean(sim - obs)\n\n    # Nash-Sutcliffe Efficiency\n    nse = 1 - (np.sum((obs - sim) ** 2) / np.sum((obs - np.mean(obs)) ** 2))\n\n    # R-squared\n    r_squared = np.corrcoef(obs, sim)[0, 1] ** 2\n\n    metrics = {\n        'RMSE': rmse,\n        'MAE': mae,\n        'Bias': bias,\n        'NSE': nse,\n        'R2': r_squared,\n        'N': len(valid_data)\n    }\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef update_nml_parameter(nml_file, section, param, value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Update a parameter in a Fortran namelist file\n\n    Parameters:\n        nml_file: Path to .nml file\n        section: Section name (e.g., 'mixing')\n        param: Parameter name\n        value: New value\n    \"\"\"\n    with open(nml_file, 'r') as f:\n        lines = f.readlines()\n\n    in_section = False\n    updated_lines = []\n    param_found = False\n\n    for line in lines:\n        if f'&{section}' in line:\n            in_section = True\n        elif line.strip() == '/':\n            in_section = False\n        elif in_section and param in line.split('=')[0]:\n            # Update the parameter\n            indent = len(line) - len(line.lstrip())\n            line = ' ' * indent + f'{param} = {value}\\n'\n            param_found = True\n\n        updated_lines.append(line)\n\n    if param_found:\n        with open(nml_file, 'w') as f:\n            f.writelines(updated_lines)\n        print(f\"Updated {section}/{param} = {value}\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"Warning: Parameter {param} not found in section {section}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"GLM Simulation for Lake Mendota\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # Check if output file exists\n    if not os.path.exists(output_nc):\n        print(f\"ERROR: Output file not found at {output_nc}\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    print(f\"Output file created: {output_nc}\")\n\n    # RMSE = {metrics['RMSE']:.3f} °C < 2.0 °C\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"✗ RMSE = {metrics['RMSE']:.3f} °C exceeds threshold of 2.0 °C\")\n        print(\"  Model calibration may be needed\")\n\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    # Save results\n    results_file = '/root/validation_results.csv'\n    results_df.to_csv(results_file, index=False)\n    print(f\"\\nResults saved to: {results_file}\")\n\n    return metrics['RMSE']\n\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    rmse = main()\n    sys.exit(0 if rmse < 2.0 else 1)\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":77,"oldLines":6,"newStart":77,"newLines":9,"lines":[" ","     # Convert to datetime","     sim_times = nc.num2date(time_values, units=time_units)","+    # Convert cftime to standard datetime","+    sim_times = [datetime(t.year, t.month, t.day, t.hour, t.minute, t.second)","+                 for t in sim_times]","     sim_times = [pd.Timestamp(t) for t in sim_times]"," ","     print(f\"Time range: {min(sim_times)} to {max(sim_times)}\")"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

Get time data\n    time_var = ds.variables['time']\n    time_units = time_var.units\n    time_values = time_var[:]\n\n    # Convert to datetime\n    sim_times = nc.num2date(time_values, units=time_units)\n    # Convert cftime to standard datetime\n    sim_times = [datetime(t.year, t.month, t.day, t.hour, t.minute, t.second)\n                 for t in sim_times]\n    sim_times = [pd.Timestamp(t) for t in sim_times]\n\n    print(f\"Time range: {min(sim_times)} to {max(sim_times)}\")\n    print(f\"Number of time steps: {len(sim_times)}\")\n\n    # Get depth and temperature data\n    z_values = ds.variables['z'][:]  # Shape: (time, z, lat, lon)\n    temp_values = ds.variables['temp'][:]  # Shape: (time, z, lat, lon)\n    ns_values = ds.variables['NS'][:]  # Remove NaN values\n    mask = ~(np.isnan(observed) | np.isnan(simulated))\n    obs_clean = np.array(observed)[mask]\n    sim_clean = np.array(simulated)[mask]\n\n    if len(obs_clean) == 0:\n        return np.nan\n\n    # Calculate RMSE\n    mse = np.mean((obs_clean - sim_clean) ** 2)\n    rmse = np.sqrt(mse)\n\n    return rmse\n\n\ndef calculate_metrics(obs_df, obs_col='temp', sim_col='simulated'):\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate comprehensive validation metrics\n\n    Returns:\n        dict: Dictionary of metrics\n    \"\"\"\n    # Remove NaN values\n    valid_data = obs_df[[obs_col, sim_col]].dropna()\n\n    if len(valid_data) == 0:\n        print(\"No valid data for metric calculation\")\n        return None\n\n    obs = valid_data[obs_col].values\n    sim = valid_data[sim_col].values\n\n    # Calculate metrics\n    rmse = np.sqrt(np.mean((obs - sim) ** 2))\n    mae = np.mean(np.abs(obs - sim))\n    bias = np.mean(sim - obs)\n\n    # Nash-Sutcliffe Efficiency\n    nse = 1 - (np.sum((obs - sim) ** 2) / np.sum((obs - np.mean(obs)) ** 2))\n\n    # R-squared\n    r_squared = np.corrcoef(obs, sim)[0, 1] ** 2\n\n    metrics = {\n        'RMSE': rmse,\n        'MAE': mae,\n        'Bias': bias,\n        'NSE': nse,\n        'R2': r_squared,\n        'N': len(valid_data)\n    }\n\n    return metrics\n\n\ndef update_nml_parameter(nml_file, section, param, value):\n    \"\"\"\n    Update a parameter in a Fortran namelist file\n\n    Parameters:\n        nml_file: Path to .nml file\n        section: Section name (e.g., 'mixing')\n        param: Parameter name\n        value: New value\n    \"\"\"\n    with open(nml_file, 'r') as f:\n        lines = f.readlines()\n\n    in_section = False\n    updated_lines = []\n    param_found = False\n\n    for line in lines:\n        if f'&{section}' in line:\n            in_section = True\n        elif line.strip() == '/':\n            in_section = False\n        elif in_section and param in line.split('=')[0]:\n            # Update the parameter\n            indent = len(line) - len(line.lstrip())\n            line = ' ' * indent + f'{param} = {value}\\n'\n            param_found = True\n\n        updated_lines.append(line)\n\n    if param_found:\n        with open(nml_file, 'w') as f:\n            f.writelines(updated_lines)\n        print(f\"Updated {section}/{param} = {value}\")\n    else:\n        print(f\"Warning: Parameter {param} not found in section {section}\")\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"GLM Simulation for Lake Mendota\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n\n    #
